--- before.sql
+++ after.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,12 @@
+WITH locally_funded AS (
+    SELECT s.School, s.DOCType,
+           f."Enrollment (K-12)" - f."Enrollment (Ages 5-17)" AS enrollment_difference
+    FROM main.schools AS s
+    JOIN main.frpm AS f ON f.CDSCode = s.CDSCode
+    WHERE s.FundingType = 'Locally funded'
+)
+SELECT School, DOCType
+FROM locally_funded
+WHERE enrollment_difference > (SELECT AVG(enrollment_difference) FROM locally_funded)
+ORDER BY School, DOCType
+LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH locally_funded AS (
    SELECT s.School, s.DOCType,
           f."Enrollment (K-12)" - f."Enrollment (Ages 5-17)" AS enrollment_difference
    FROM main.schools AS s
    JOIN main.frpm AS f ON f.CDSCode = s.CDSCode
    WHERE s.FundingType = 'Locally funded'
)
SELECT School, DOCType
FROM locally_funded
WHERE enrollment_difference > (SELECT AVG(enrollment_difference) FROM locally_funded)
ORDER BY School, DOCType
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    1.0 * COUNT(School) FILTER (WHERE DOCType = 'Unified School District')
    / NULLIF(COUNT(School) FILTER (WHERE DOCType = 'Elementary School District'), 0) AS ratio
FROM main.schools
WHERE County = 'Orange'
  AND StatusType = 'Merged'
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    1.0 * COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DOCType = 'Unified School District')
    / NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DOCType = 'Elementary School District'), 0) AS ratio
FROM main.schools
WHERE County = 'Orange'
  AND StatusType = 'Merged'
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT MAX(CAST(frameVersion AS INTEGER)) - MIN(CAST(frameVersion AS INTEGER)) AS span_years
FROM main.cards
WHERE name = 'Tarmogoyf'
  AND frameVersion GLOB '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]'
LIMIT 1

  "evidence": "\"Boros watermark\" refers to the guild watermark appearing on cards of the Boros guild; \"printed in a foreign language\" means the card was printed in any language other than English."

Final SQL / answer

WITH type_counts AS (
    SELECT c.types AS card_type, COUNT(*) AS card_count
    FROM cards AS c
    WHERE c.watermark = 'boros'
      AND c.uuid IN (
          SELECT f.uuid
          FROM foreign_data AS f
          WHERE f.language <> 'English' AND f.language <> ''
      )
    GROUP BY c.types
)
SELECT card_type, card_count
FROM type_counts
WHERE card_count = (SELECT MAX(card_count) FROM type_counts)
ORDER BY card_type
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH type_counts AS (
    SELECT c.types AS card_type, COUNT(*) AS card_count
    FROM cards AS c
    WHERE c.watermark = 'boros'
      AND c.uuid IN (
          SELECT f.uuid
          FROM foreign_data AS f
          WHERE f.language <> 'English'
      )
    GROUP BY c.types
)
SELECT card_type, card_count
FROM type_counts
WHERE card_count = (SELECT MAX(card_count) FROM type_counts)
ORDER BY card_type
LIMIT 1000

For every card that has a boros watermark and a Japanese printing, please give me: the Japanese card name, the English card type, and the total number of official rulings recorded for that card. A card is identified by its Japanese name together with its English card type - printings that share both count as one card, and if either differs they should be listed as separate cards. The ruling total should add up all ruling entries across every printing of that card, and if a card has no rulings, list it with a total of 0.",
  "evidence": "Boros watermark means the watermark is marked 'boros' on the card; a Japanese printing is the foreign data entry in Japanese; rulings are the official rules clarifications recorded for a card."

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    j.name AS japanese_name,
    c.type AS english_card_type,
    COUNT(DISTINCT r.id) AS total_rulings
FROM foreign_data AS j
JOIN cards AS c ON c.uuid = j.uuid
LEFT JOIN rulings AS r ON r.uuid = c.uuid
WHERE j.language = 'Japanese'
GROUP BY j.name, c.type
HAVING MAX(CASE WHEN c.watermark = 'boros' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 1
ORDER BY total_rulings DESC, j.name, c.type
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    j.name AS japanese_name,
    c.type AS english_card_type,
    COUNT(DISTINCT r.id) AS total_rulings
FROM main.foreign_data AS j
JOIN main.cards AS c ON c.uuid = j.uuid
LEFT JOIN main.rulings AS r ON r.uuid = c.uuid
WHERE j.language = 'Japanese'
GROUP BY j.name, c.type
HAVING MAX(CASE WHEN c.watermark = 'boros' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 1
ORDER BY total_rulings DESC, japanese_name, english_card_type
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH editor_counts AS (
    SELECT h.UserId, COUNT(DISTINCT h.RevisionGUID) AS edit_comment_count
    FROM main.posts AS p
    JOIN main.postHistory AS h ON h.PostId = p.Id
    WHERE p.Title = 'Why square the difference instead of taking the absolute value in standard deviation?'
      AND TRIM(h.Comment) <> ''
    GROUP BY h.UserId
)
SELECT u.DisplayName, e.edit_comment_count
FROM editor_counts AS e
JOIN main.users AS u ON u.Id = e.UserId
WHERE e.edit_comment_count = (SELECT MAX(edit_comment_count) FROM editor_counts)
ORDER BY u.DisplayName
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH editor_counts AS (
    SELECT h.UserId, COUNT(DISTINCT h.RevisionGUID) AS edit_comment_count
    FROM main.posts AS p
    JOIN main.postHistory AS h ON h.PostId = p.Id
    WHERE p.Title = 'Why square the difference instead of taking the absolute value in standard deviation?'
      AND TRIM(h.Comment) <> ''
    GROUP BY h.UserId
)
SELECT u.DisplayName, e.edit_comment_count
FROM editor_counts AS e
JOIN main.users AS u ON u.Id = e.UserId
WHERE e.edit_comment_count = (SELECT MAX(edit_comment_count) FROM editor_counts)
ORDER BY u.DisplayName
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH annual_totals AS (
    SELECT c.Segment, c.CustomerID,
           SUM(CAST(ROUND(y.Consumption * 100) AS INTEGER)) AS total_hundredths
    FROM main.customers AS c
    JOIN main.yearmonth AS y ON y.CustomerID = c.CustomerID
    WHERE c.Currency = 'CZK'
      AND c.Segment IN ('SME', 'LAM', 'KAM')
      AND y.Date BETWEEN '201301' AND '201312'
      AND y.Consumption IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY c.Segment, c.CustomerID
),
ranked AS (
    SELECT annual_totals.*,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
               PARTITION BY Segment ORDER BY total_hundredths, CustomerID
           ) AS segment_rank
    FROM annual_totals
),
winners AS (
    SELECT Segment, CustomerID, total_hundredths
    FROM ranked
    WHERE segment_rank = 1
)
SELECT sme.CustomerID AS sme_customer_id, sme.total_hundredths / 100.0 AS sme_total,
       lam.CustomerID AS lam_customer_id, lam.total_hundredths / 100.0 AS lam_total,
       kam.CustomerID AS kam_customer_id, kam.total_hundredths / 100.0 AS kam_total,
       ROUND((sme.total_hundredths - lam.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS sme_minus_lam,
       ROUND((lam.total_hundredths - kam.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS lam_minus_kam,
       ROUND((kam.total_hundredths - sme.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS kam_minus_sme
FROM winners AS sme
CROSS JOIN winners AS lam
CROSS JOIN winners AS kam
WHERE sme.Segment = 'SME' AND lam.Segment = 'LAM' AND kam.Segment = 'KAM'
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH customer_totals AS (
    SELECT c.Segment, c.CustomerID,
           SUM(CAST(ROUND(y.Consumption * 100) AS INTEGER)) AS total_hundredths
    FROM main.customers AS c
    JOIN main.yearmonth AS y ON y.CustomerID = c.CustomerID
    WHERE c.Currency = 'CZK'
      AND c.Segment IN ('SME', 'LAM', 'KAM')
      AND y.Date BETWEEN '201301' AND '201312'
      AND y.Consumption IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY c.Segment, c.CustomerID
),
ranked AS (
    SELECT customer_totals.*,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
               PARTITION BY Segment
               ORDER BY total_hundredths, CustomerID
           ) AS position
    FROM customer_totals
),
winners AS (
    SELECT Segment, CustomerID, total_hundredths
    FROM ranked
    WHERE position = 1
)
SELECT sme.CustomerID AS sme_customer_id,
       lam.CustomerID AS lam_customer_id,
       kam.CustomerID AS kam_customer_id,
       ROUND((sme.total_hundredths - lam.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS sme_minus_lam,
       ROUND((lam.total_hundredths - kam.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS lam_minus_kam,
       ROUND((kam.total_hundredths - sme.total_hundredths) / 100.0, 2) AS kam_minus_sme
FROM winners AS sme
CROSS JOIN winners AS lam
CROSS JOIN winners AS kam
WHERE sme.Segment = 'SME' AND lam.Segment = 'LAM' AND kam.Segment = 'KAM'
LIMIT 1

This changes neither the source values nor the DB connection; run_sql remains required.\n\ndecimal_bounds AS (\n  SELECT group_id, scaled_value,\n    COUNT(scaled_value) OVER g AS n,\n    SUM(CASE WHEN scaled_value IS NOT NULL AND typeof(scaled_value) <> 'integer' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) OVER g AS invalid_count,\n    SUM(CASE WHEN typeof(scaled_value) = 'integer' AND scaled_value NOT BETWEEN -90071992547409 AND 90071992547409 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) OVER g AS oversized_count,\n    TOTAL(CASE WHEN typeof(scaled_value) = 'integer' AND scaled_value BETWEEN -90071992547409 AND 90071992547409 THEN ABS(scaled_value) ELSE 0 END) OVER g AS abs_total\n  FROM decimal_input\n  WINDOW g AS (PARTITION BY group_id)\n), decimal_totals AS (\n  SELECT group_id, n, invalid_count,\n    CASE WHEN invalid_count > 0 THEN 'invalid_input'\n      WHEN n = 0 THEN 'empty'\n      WHEN oversized_count > 0 OR abs_total > 90071992547409\n        OR n > 90071992547409 THEN 'overflow'\n      ELSE 'ok' END AS status,\n    SUM(CASE WHEN invalid_count = 0 AND oversized_count = 0\n      AND abs_total <= 90071992547409 AND n <= 90071992547409\n      THEN scaled_value END) AS scaled_sum\n  FROM decimal_bounds\n  GROUP BY group_id\n), decimal_fraction AS (\n  SELECT group_id, n, invalid_count, status,\n    scaled_sum * 100 AS numerator, 100 AS denominator\n  FROM decimal_totals\n), decimal_rounded AS (\n  SELECT group_id, n, invalid_count, status,\n    CASE WHEN status = 'ok' THEN numerator / denominator\n      + CASE WHEN ABS(numerator % denominator) >= (denominator + 1) / 2\n        THEN CASE WHEN numerator < 0 THEN -1 ELSE 1 END ELSE 0 END END AS rounded_units\n  FROM decimal_fraction\n), decimal_result AS (\n  SELECT *, rounded_units * 1.0 / 100 AS rounded_value\n  FROM decimal_rounded\n)"

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS transaction_count
FROM main.trans AS t
WHERE (t.k_symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(t.k_symbol) = '')
  AND t.account_id IN (
      SELECT d.account_id
      FROM main.disp AS d
      JOIN main.client AS c ON c.client_id = d.client_id
      WHERE d.type = 'OWNER'
        AND c.gender = 'M'
        AND c.birth_date >= '1974-01-01'
        AND c.birth_date < '1977-01-01'
  )
LIMIT 1

+        COALESCE(l.loan_count, 0) AS loan_count,
+        COALESCE(l.total_borrowed, 0) AS total_borrowed,

+    LEFT JOIN account_loans AS l ON l.account_id = d.account_id

Final SQL / answer

WITH account_loans AS (
    SELECT account_id, COUNT(*) AS loan_count, SUM(amount) AS total_amount_borrowed
    FROM loan
    GROUP BY account_id
),
profiles AS (
    SELECT c.client_id, c.gender, c.birth_date, k.type AS card_type,
           CAST(strftime('%Y', k.issued) AS INTEGER)
               - CAST(strftime('%Y', c.birth_date) AS INTEGER) AS age_at_issue,
           COALESCE(l.loan_count, 0) AS loan_count,
           COALESCE(l.total_amount_borrowed, 0) AS total_amount_borrowed,
           d.A2 AS district_name, d.A11 AS district_average_salary
    FROM card AS k
    JOIN disp AS p ON p.disp_id = k.disp_id
    JOIN client AS c ON c.client_id = p.client_id
    JOIN district AS d ON d.district_id = c.district_id
    LEFT JOIN account_loans AS l ON l.account_id = p.account_id
    WHERE k.issued >= '1994-01-01' AND k.issued < '1995-01-01'
)
SELECT client_id, gender, birth_date, card_type, age_at_issue,
       loan_count, total_amount_borrowed,
       CASE
           WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue < 30 THEN 'Young borrower'
           WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue >= 30 THEN 'Mature borrower'
           ELSE 'Non-borrower'
       END AS borrower_classification,
       district_name, district_average_salary,
       RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY gender ORDER BY age_at_issue DESC) AS age_rank_within_gender
FROM profiles
ORDER BY client_id
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH account_loans AS (
    SELECT account_id, COUNT(*) AS loan_count, SUM(amount) AS total_borrowed
    FROM main.loan
    GROUP BY account_id
),
profiles AS (
    SELECT
        cl.client_id,
        cl.gender,
        cl.birth_date,
        c.type AS card_type,
        CAST(strftime('%Y', c.issued) AS INTEGER)
            - CAST(strftime('%Y', cl.birth_date) AS INTEGER) AS age_at_issue,
        COALESCE(l.loan_count, 0) AS loan_count,
        COALESCE(l.total_borrowed, 0) AS total_borrowed,
        dt.A2 AS district_name,
        dt.A11 AS district_average_salary
    FROM main.card AS c
    JOIN main.disp AS d ON d.disp_id = c.disp_id
    JOIN main.client AS cl ON cl.client_id = d.client_id
    JOIN main.district AS dt ON dt.district_id = cl.district_id
    LEFT JOIN account_loans AS l ON l.account_id = d.account_id
    WHERE c.issued >= '1994-01-01' AND c.issued < '1995-01-01'
)
SELECT
    client_id,
    gender,
    birth_date,
    card_type,
    age_at_issue,
    loan_count,
    total_borrowed,
    CASE
        WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue < 30 THEN 'Young borrower'
        WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue >= 30 THEN 'Mature borrower'
        ELSE 'Non-borrower'
    END AS borrower_classification,
    district_name,
    district_average_salary,
    RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY gender ORDER BY age_at_issue DESC) AS age_rank_within_gender
FROM profiles
ORDER BY client_id
LIMIT 1000

{
  "instance_id": "bird228",
  "db": "financial",
  "question": "For the year-end review of my branch at location 1: how many accounts that were on the branch's books as of 31 December 1995 hold an active loan contract? I want a single integer, nothing else. An account counts only if it belonged to the branch on that date, and only contracts that are still active by their repayment status count - statuses A and B are finished contracts and shouldn't be included.",
  "evidence": "Loan status C means an active contract in good standing; loan status D means an active contract with the client in debt. Both are active contracts."

+SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT a.account_id) AS account_count
+FROM main.account AS a
+JOIN main.loan AS l ON l.account_id = a.account_id
+WHERE a.district_id = 1
+  AND a.date <= '1995-12-31'
+  AND l.status IN ('C', 'D')
+LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT a.account_id) AS account_count
FROM main.account AS a
JOIN main.loan AS l ON l.account_id = a.account_id
WHERE a.district_id = 1
  AND a.date <= '1995-12-31'
  AND l.status IN ('C', 'D')
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'Peter') AS Peter,
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'John') AS John
FROM main.results AS r
JOIN main.drivers AS d ON d.driverId = r.driverId
WHERE r.position = 1
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'Peter') AS Peter,
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'John') AS John
FROM main.results AS r
JOIN main.drivers AS d ON d.driverId = r.driverId
WHERE r.position = 1
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH career_results AS (
    SELECT x.driverId, x.raceId, x.position, r.name, r.date
    FROM results AS x
    JOIN races AS r ON r.raceId = x.raceId
    WHERE r.year BETWEEN 1950 AND 2017
),
first_win AS (
    SELECT d.driverId,
           d.forename || ' ' || d.surname AS full_name,
           d.nationality,
           w.name AS first_win_race,
           w.date AS first_win_date
    FROM career_results AS w
    JOIN drivers AS d ON d.driverId = w.driverId
    WHERE w.position = 1 AND d.dob IS NOT NULL
    ORDER BY d.dob, full_name, d.driverId, w.date, w.name
    LIMIT 1
)
SELECT f.full_name, f.nationality, f.first_win_race, f.first_win_date,
       (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT c.raceId)
        FROM career_results AS c
        WHERE c.driverId = f.driverId) AS career_starts
FROM first_win AS f
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH members_per_major AS (
    SELECT m.department, m.major_id, m.major_name, COUNT(r.member_id) AS member_count
    FROM main.major AS m
    LEFT JOIN main.member AS r ON r.link_to_major = m.major_id
    WHERE m.department IN (
        SELECT department
        FROM main.major
        WHERE college = 'College of Humanities and Social Sciences'
    )
    GROUP BY m.department, m.major_id, m.major_name
)
SELECT department,
       json_group_array(major_name ORDER BY major_name, major_id) AS majors_offered,
       json_group_array(major_name ORDER BY major_name, major_id)
           FILTER (WHERE member_count > 0) AS majors_held_by_members,
       ROUND(AVG(member_count), 2) AS average_members_per_major
FROM members_per_major
GROUP BY department
ORDER BY department
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH members_per_major AS (
    SELECT m.department, m.major_name, COUNT(r.member_id) AS member_count
    FROM main.major AS m
    LEFT JOIN main.member AS r ON r.link_to_major = m.major_id
    WHERE m.department IN (
        SELECT department
        FROM main.major
        WHERE college = 'College of Humanities and Social Sciences'
    )
    GROUP BY m.department, m.major_name, m.major_id
    ORDER BY m.department, m.major_name, m.major_id
)
SELECT department,
       JSON_GROUP_ARRAY(major_name) AS majors_offered,
       JSON_GROUP_ARRAY(major_name) FILTER (WHERE member_count > 0) AS majors_held,
       ROUND(AVG(member_count), 2) AS average_members_per_major
FROM members_per_major
GROUP BY department
ORDER BY department
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT e.expense_date, SUM(e.cost) AS total_cost
FROM main.expense AS e
WHERE e.expense_date = (
    SELECT MAX(vp.expense_date)
    FROM main.expense AS vp
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = vp.link_to_member
    WHERE m.position = 'Vice President'
)
GROUP BY e.expense_date
LIMIT 1

bird321: 0 -> 1
{
  "instance_id": "bird321",
  "db": "student_club",
  "question": "I want to review what the money actually went on for the well-funded events, by venue. For events that had a budget line of more than forty dollars, how many different things were bought for them at each location? Please give me, for each location, the location of the event and the number of different things bought for such events at that location, and nothing else. Note that a budget line of more than forty dollars means a single budgeted amount strictly over 40 dollars, and all qualifying events held at a location should be pooled together.",
  "evidence": ""
}

SQL diff

--- before.sql
+++ after.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+SELECT
+    e.location,
+    COUNT(DISTINCT x.expense_description) AS distinct_things_bought
+FROM event AS e
+JOIN budget AS b ON b.link_to_event = e.event_id
+LEFT JOIN expense AS x ON x.link_to_budget = b.budget_id
+WHERE e.event_id IN (
+    SELECT q.link_to_event
+    FROM budget AS q
+    WHERE q.amount > 40
+)
+GROUP BY e.location
+LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    e.location,
    COUNT(DISTINCT x.expense_description) AS distinct_things_bought
FROM event AS e
JOIN budget AS b ON b.link_to_event = e.event_id
LEFT JOIN expense AS x ON x.link_to_budget = b.budget_id
WHERE e.event_id IN (
    SELECT q.link_to_event
    FROM budget AS q
    WHERE q.amount > 40
)
GROUP BY e.location
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE items(expense_id, item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT e.expense_id,
           NULL,
           CASE WHEN e.expense_description GLOB 'Bakery - *'
                THEN substr(e.expense_description, length('Bakery - ') + 1)
                ELSE e.expense_description
           END || ','
    FROM main.expense AS e
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member
    WHERE m.first_name = 'Sacha' AND m.last_name = 'Harrison'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT expense_id,
           trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1)),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
)
SELECT item_name, COUNT(DISTINCT expense_id) AS purchase_count
FROM items
WHERE item_name <> ''
GROUP BY item_name
ORDER BY purchase_count DESC, item_name
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE items(expense_id, item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT e.expense_id,
           NULL,
           CASE WHEN e.expense_description GLOB 'Bakery - *'
                THEN substr(e.expense_description, length('Bakery - ') + 1)
                ELSE e.expense_description
           END || ','
    FROM main.expense AS e
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member
    WHERE m.first_name = 'Sacha' AND m.last_name = 'Harrison'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT expense_id,
           trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1)),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
)
SELECT item_name, COUNT(DISTINCT expense_id) AS times_bought
FROM items
WHERE item_name <> ''
GROUP BY item_name
ORDER BY times_bought DESC, item_name
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE period_expenses AS (
    SELECT e.link_to_member AS member_id, e.expense_description, e.cost
    FROM main.expense AS e
    WHERE e.expense_date >= '2019-10-01'
      AND e.expense_date < '2019-12-01'
),
items(member_id, item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT member_id, NULL, expense_description || ','
    FROM period_expenses
    UNION ALL
    SELECT member_id,
           LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTR(remaining, 1, INSTR(remaining, ',') - 1))),
           SUBSTR(remaining, INSTR(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
),
kind_counts AS (
    SELECT member_id, COUNT(DISTINCT item_name) AS kind_count
    FROM items
    WHERE item_name <> ''
    GROUP BY member_id
),
spending AS (
    SELECT member_id, ROUND(SUM(cost), 2) AS total_spending_dollars
    FROM period_expenses
    GROUP BY member_id
)
SELECT m.first_name || ' ' || m.last_name AS full_name,
       k.kind_count,
       s.total_spending_dollars
FROM kind_counts AS k
JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = k.member_id
JOIN spending AS s ON s.member_id = k.member_id
WHERE k.kind_count = (SELECT MAX(kind_count) FROM kind_counts)
ORDER BY full_name, m.member_id
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE period_expenses AS (
    SELECT e.link_to_member AS member_id, e.expense_description, e.cost
    FROM main.expense AS e
    WHERE e.expense_date >= '2019-10-01'
      AND e.expense_date < '2019-12-01'
),
items(member_id, item, remaining) AS (
    SELECT member_id, NULL, expense_description || ','
    FROM period_expenses
    UNION ALL
    SELECT member_id,
           lower(trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1))),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
),
kind_counts AS (
    SELECT member_id, COUNT(DISTINCT NULLIF(item, '')) AS kind_count
    FROM items
    GROUP BY member_id
),
spending AS (
    SELECT member_id,
           SUM(CAST(ROUND(cost * 100) AS INTEGER)) / 100.0 AS total_spending_dollars
    FROM period_expenses
    GROUP BY member_id
),
ranked AS (
    SELECT m.first_name || ' ' || m.last_name AS full_name,
           k.kind_count,
           s.total_spending_dollars,
           RANK() OVER (ORDER BY k.kind_count DESC) AS kind_rank
    FROM main.member AS m
    JOIN kind_counts AS k ON k.member_id = m.member_id
    JOIN spending AS s ON s.member_id = m.member_id
)
SELECT full_name, kind_count, total_spending_dollars
FROM ranked
WHERE kind_rank = 1
ORDER BY full_name
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS character_count
FROM superhero AS s
LEFT JOIN race AS r ON r.id = s.race_id
WHERE s.publisher_id IN (
    SELECT a.publisher_id
    FROM superhero AS a
    WHERE a.superhero_name = 'A-Bomb'
)
AND (r.race IS NULL OR r.race = '-')
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS publisher_count
FROM main.superhero AS s
JOIN main.publisher AS p ON p.id = s.publisher_id
WHERE s.height_cm > 200
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS superhero_count
FROM main.superhero AS s
JOIN main.alignment AS a ON a.id = s.alignment_id
JOIN main.publisher AS p ON p.id = s.publisher_id
WHERE a.alignment = 'Good'
  AND p.publisher_name <> 'DC Comics'
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH patient_counts AS (
    SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, COUNT(l.Date) AS test_dates
    FROM main.Patient AS p
    LEFT JOIN main.Laboratory AS l ON l.ID = p.ID
    WHERE p.SEX IN ('F', 'M')
      AND p.ID NOT IN (
          SELECT e.ID
          FROM main.Examination AS e
          WHERE e.ID IS NOT NULL
      )
    GROUP BY p.ID, p.SEX
)
SELECT ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN SEX = 'F' THEN test_dates END), 2) AS F,
       ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN SEX = 'M' THEN test_dates END), 2) AS M
FROM patient_counts
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH patient_counts AS (
    SELECT p.SEX, COUNT(l."Date") AS test_dates
    FROM main.Patient AS p
    LEFT JOIN main.Laboratory AS l ON l.ID = p.ID
    WHERE p.SEX IN ('F', 'M')
      AND p.ID NOT IN (
          SELECT e.ID
          FROM main.Examination AS e
          WHERE e.ID IS NOT NULL
      )
    GROUP BY p.ID, p.SEX
)
SELECT ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN SEX = 'F' THEN test_dates END), 2) AS "F",
       ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN SEX = 'M' THEN test_dates END), 2) AS "M"
FROM patient_counts
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH platelet_history AS (
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM main.Laboratory AS l
        WHERE l.ID = p.ID
          AND l.PLT > 100
          AND l.PLT < 400
    ) AS had_normal_platelets
    FROM main.Patient AS p
    WHERE p.Diagnosis IN ('RA, SJS', 'RA,SJS', 'SJS, RA', 'SJS  RA')
)
SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal_platelets = 1) AS ever_normal_platelets,
       COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal_platelets = 0) AS never_normal_platelets
FROM platelet_history
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH patient_history AS (
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM main.Laboratory AS l
        WHERE l.ID = p.ID
          AND l.PLT > 100
          AND l.PLT < 400
    ) AS had_normal
    FROM main.Patient AS p
    WHERE p.Diagnosis IN ('RA, SJS', 'RA,SJS', 'SJS, RA', 'SJS  RA')
)
SELECT
    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal = 1) AS ever_normal,
    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal = 0) AS never_normal
FROM patient_history
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH exams AS (
    SELECT e.ID, e."Examination Date", e.Symptoms,
           REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(TRIM(e.Diagnosis), '->', ','), '+', ','), ', ', ',') AS diseases
    FROM Examination AS e
    WHERE TRIM(e.Symptoms) <> ''
)
SELECT e.Symptoms, p.Diagnosis AS overall_diagnosis
FROM exams AS e
JOIN Patient AS p ON p.ID = e.ID
WHERE (',' || e.diseases || ',') LIKE '%,SLE,%'
  AND p.Birthday IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY p.Birthday DESC, p.ID ASC, e."Examination Date" DESC
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT e.Symptoms, p.Diagnosis AS overall_diagnosis
FROM Examination AS e
JOIN Patient AS p ON p.ID = e.ID
WHERE TRIM(e.Symptoms) <> ''
  AND p.Birthday IS NOT NULL
  AND (',' || REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(e.Diagnosis, ' ', ''), '+', ','), '->', ',') || ',')
      LIKE '%,SLE,%'
ORDER BY p.Birthday DESC, p.ID ASC, e."Examination Date" DESC
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH requested_types(bond_type, sort_order) AS (
    VALUES ('-', 1), ('=', 2), ('#', 3)
),
carbon_bonds AS (
    SELECT b.bond_type, b.bond_id
    FROM main.bond AS b
    JOIN main.molecule AS m ON m.molecule_id = b.molecule_id
    JOIN main.connected AS c ON c.bond_id = b.bond_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a1 ON a1.atom_id = c.atom_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a2 ON a2.atom_id = c.atom_id2
    WHERE m.label = '+'
      AND a1.element = 'c'
      AND a2.element = 'c'
)
SELECT t.bond_type, COUNT(DISTINCT b.bond_id) AS bond_count
FROM requested_types AS t
LEFT JOIN carbon_bonds AS b ON b.bond_type = t.bond_type
GROUP BY t.sort_order, t.bond_type
ORDER BY t.sort_order
LIMIT 3

Final SQL / answer

WITH requested_types(bond_type, sort_order) AS (
    VALUES ('-', 1), ('=', 2), ('#', 3)
),
bond_counts AS (
    SELECT b.bond_type, COUNT(DISTINCT b.bond_id) AS bond_count
    FROM bond AS b
    JOIN molecule AS m ON m.molecule_id = b.molecule_id
    JOIN connected AS c ON c.bond_id = b.bond_id
    JOIN atom AS a1 ON a1.atom_id = c.atom_id
    JOIN atom AS a2 ON a2.atom_id = c.atom_id2
    WHERE m.label = '+'
      AND a1.element = 'c'
      AND a2.element = 'c'
    GROUP BY b.bond_type
)
SELECT t.bond_type, COALESCE(bc.bond_count, 0) AS bond_count
FROM requested_types AS t
LEFT JOIN bond_counts AS bc ON bc.bond_type = t.bond_type
ORDER BY t.sort_order
LIMIT 1000
